function setCookie(cname, cvalue, exminutes , path) {
	if(typeof path === "undefined") {
		path = "/";
	}
	var d = new Date();
	d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exminutes * 60 * 1000));
	var expires = "expires="+ d.toUTCString();
	document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";path=" + path;
}

function getCookie(cname) {
	var name = cname + "=";
	try {
		var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
	} catch(e) {
		var decodedCookie = document.cookie;
	}
	var ca = decodedCookie.split(";");
	for(var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
		var c = ca[i];
		while (c.charAt(0) == " ") {
			c = c.substring(1);
		}
		if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
			return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
		}
	}
	return "";
}

        (function() {
            const root = document.documentElement;
            
            // Désactiver le mode clair sur les pages /telecharger, mais conserver l'information dans le localStorage
            if (/^\/telecharger(\/.*)?$/.test(window.location.pathname)) {
                root.removeAttribute("data-color-scheme");
                return;
            }
            
            // Appliquer le mode clair s'il est sélectionné dans le localStorage
            const selectedColor = localStorage.getItem("user-color-scheme");
            
            if (selectedColor === "light") {
                root.dataset.colorScheme = "light";
            }
            
            // Une fois le DOM chargé, ajouter l'écouteur pour le bouton de bascule
            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
                const themeToggleButton = document.getElementById("theme-toggle-button");
                
                if (themeToggleButton) {
                    themeToggleButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
                        const isLightMode = root.dataset.colorScheme === "light";
                        if (isLightMode) {
                            // Supprimer le mode clair
                            root.removeAttribute("data-color-scheme");
                            localStorage.removeItem("user-color-scheme");
                        } else {
                            // Ajouter le mode clair
                            root.dataset.colorScheme = "light";
                            localStorage.setItem("user-color-scheme", "light");
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        })();

function shift_sticky_ad(selector) {
	jQuery(window).on('scroll', function() {
		var $_sticky = jQuery(selector).first();
		if(typeof $_sticky !== 'undefined' && $_sticky) {
			var $_placeholders = jQuery('.placeholder-pub_dfp.placeholder-filled-with-pub, .rwad-with-placeholder.rwad-placeholder-filled');
			var offset_sticky = $_sticky.offset();
			var height_sticky = $_sticky.outerHeight();
			if(typeof offset_sticky !== 'undefined' && offset_sticky) {
				$_placeholders.each(function() {
					var offset = jQuery(this).offset();
					var sticky_h = offset_sticky.top + height_sticky;
					var placeholder_h = offset.top + jQuery(this).outerHeight();
					// if sticky is over pub placeholder
					if(offset.top <= sticky_h && sticky_h < placeholder_h) {
						jQuery(this).find('.pub_dfp').css('top', height_sticky + 'px');
					} else {
						jQuery(this).find('.pub_dfp').css('top', '0');
					}
				});
			}
		}
	});
}

function check_slot_rendering(){
	jQuery(document).ready(function() {
		function hideAdPlaceholder(element) {
			if(site_config_js.devs.placeholder_pub_dfp_18304) {
				var placeholder = element.closest('.placeholder-pub_dfp, .rwad-with-placeholder');
				if(placeholder){
					placeholder.addClass('placeholder-filled-with-pub');
					var unsetAdHeight = typeof(site_config_js.unsetAdHeight) === "function" ? site_config_js.unsetAdHeight(placeholder) : true;
					if ((site_config_js.responsive_placeholder_2698) && unsetAdHeight) {
						placeholder.css('minHeight','unset');
						element.css('minHeight','unset');
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if( typeof window.googletag !== 'undefined' && window.googletag){
			googletag.cmd.push(function() {
				googletag.pubads().addEventListener('slotRenderEnded', function(event) {
					var adPlaceholderFilled = false;
					// Récupérer l'id du div contenant la pub
					var id_div = event.slot.getSlotId().getDomId();
					var element = jQuery('#' + id_div);
					// Au cas on a l'habillage masqué
					var mask_cdt = (
						(typeof(site_config_js.dfp_div_ids.masthead_haut) !== 'undefined' && id_div == site_config_js.dfp_div_ids.masthead_haut) ||
						(is_mobile && typeof(site_config_js.dfp_div_ids.sticky_banner_top) !== 'undefined' && id_div == site_config_js.dfp_div_ids.sticky_banner_top)
					);
					if(mask_cdt) {
						var mask_id = 'rw_skin_clickMask';
						jQuery('.' + mask_id + '_to_remove').remove();
						if(typeof element.prev()[0] !== 'undefined' && element.prev()[0] == jQuery('#' + mask_id)[0]) {
							adPlaceholderFilled = true;
							jQuery('#' + mask_id).addClass(mask_id + '_to_remove');
							var maskChildNewTop = jQuery('#' + mask_id)[0].getBoundingClientRect().top + jQuery(document).scrollTop();
							jQuery('#' + mask_id + ' a').css('top', maskChildNewTop + 'px');
							jQuery('body')
								.addClass(mask_id + '_habillage')
								.addClass('skin_size_1000')
								.addClass('rw_dfp_skin')
								.css('backgroundPosition', 'center ' + maskChildNewTop + 'px');
								if (is_mobile) {
									var placeholder = element.closest('.placeholder-pub_dfp, .rwad-with-placeholder');
									if (placeholder) {
										placeholder.css('flexDirection','column');
										placeholder.css('minHeight','unset');
										element.css('minHeight','unset');
									}
								}
						}
					}
					if(!event.isEmpty) {
						var classes_to_add = 'filled-with-pub';
						// Récupérer le width de la pub
						var width_pub = event.size ? event.size[0] : null;
						if(width_pub) {
						    classes_to_add += ' has-pub-' + width_pub;
						}
						element.addClass(classes_to_add);
						adPlaceholderFilled = true;
					}
					
					if(adPlaceholderFilled) {
						hideAdPlaceholder(element);
					}
				});
			});
		}
	});
}

function get_utm_value_filtered(utm_name){
	const slotsSource = {
	    'Snap': 'paid_snap',
	    'TikTok': 'paid_tiktok',
	    'google': 'paid_google',
	    'RevC': 'paid_revcontent',
	    'media10': 'paid_media10',
	    'cpttraff': 'paid_captain',
	    'PT_MF': 'paid_powerspace',
	    'CT_MF': 'paid_powerspace',
	    'PA_MF': 'paid_powerspace',
	    'twitter': 'paid_twitter',
	    'IVIDENCE': 'paid_ividence',
	    'supremedia': 'paid_supremedia',
	    'captaintraffic': 'paid_captain',
	    'Taboola_mobile': 'paid_taboola',
	    'Mobile_twitter': 'paid_twitter',
	    'pausecafein': 'paid_pausecafein',
	    'Outbrain_mobile': 'paid_outbrain',
	    'Mobile_facebook': 'paid_facebook',
	    'Mobile_instagram': 'paid_instagram',
	    'sprmdfb': 'paid_supremedia',
	};
	if (site_config_js.add_new_elements_to_slotsSource === true) {
		slotsSource['taboola_desktop'] = slotsSource['Taboola_tablet'] = 'paid_taboola';
		slotsSource['Outbrain_desktop'] = slotsSource['Outbrain_tablet'] = 'paid_outbrain';
		slotsSource['Desktop_facebook'] = slotsSource['Tablet_facebook'] = 'paid_facebook';
	}
	if (site_config_js.devs.add_new_key_values_3545){
		slotsSource['trc.taboola.com'] = slotsSource['api.taboola.com'] = slotsSource['Taboola'] = 'paid_taboola';
		slotsSource['Outbrain'] = slotsSource['outbrain.com'] = slotsSource['paid.outbrain.com'] = slotsSource['traffic.outbrain.com'] = slotsSource['Outbrain_desktop'] = 'paid_outbrain';
		slotsSource['captaintraffi4'] = 'paid_captain';
	}

	const lowercaseSlotsSource = Object.keys(slotsSource).reduce((acc, key) => {
	    acc[key.toLowerCase()] = slotsSource[key];
	    return acc;
	}, {});

	var queryString = window.location.search;
	var queryParams = new URLSearchParams(queryString);
	var utm_value = '';
	var utm_value_filtered = '';

	if (queryParams.has(utm_name)) {
		utm_value = queryParams.get(utm_name);
	}
	if (lowercaseSlotsSource[utm_value.toLowerCase()] !== undefined && site_config_js.devs.display_GAM_keyvalue_parsource_2493) {
		utm_value_filtered =  lowercaseSlotsSource[utm_value.toLowerCase()];
	}
	return utm_value_filtered;
}

	if (queryParams.has('utm_source')) {
		utm_value_sourcetraffic = 'paid';
	}
	return utm_value_sourcetraffic;

|     <script>
|       data-rocket-type="text/javascript"
|       data-rocketid="ip_address"
|       type="rocketlazyloadscript"
|       "
            function fetch_country_and_execute(callback, restricted_countries = []) {
                const cacheKey = 'user_country';
                const cacheDuration = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 30 days
                const now = Date.now();

                let cached = null;
                try {
                    cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(cacheKey));
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn("Invalid localStorage content for user_country");
                }

                if (cached && cached.country && cached.timestamp && (now - cached.timestamp) < cacheDuration) {
                    console.log('working with the local storage');
                    handleCountry(cached.country);
                    return;
                }

                fetch('https://ipinfo.io/json')
                    .then(res => res.json())
                    .then(data => {
                        if (data && data.country) {
                            const country = data.country;
                            console.log('working with the API');
                            localStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify({
                                country,
                                timestamp: now
                            }));
                            handleCountry(country);
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(error => {
                        console.error("An error occurred while fetching the country:", error);
                    });

                function handleCountry(country) {
                    if (!restricted_countries.includes(country)) {
                        if (typeof callback === 'function') {
                            callback();
                        } else {
                            console.error("Provided callback is not a function!");
                        }
                    } else {
                        document.body.classList.add("country-restricted");
                        console.log("Country is restricted:", country);
                    }
                }
            }

                }

                function merge_key_in_dataSlayer(index,attr_key,new_data) {

                    if (
                        typeof attr_key === "string" && attr_key != "" &&
                        typeof window[dataLaye_name][index][attr_key] === "object" && 
                        typeof new_data[attr_key] === "object"
                    ) {
